1. Moving to India to UAE

5:52
So yes, it is 100% possible for you to get a job without being physically present in the UAE. But terms and conditions obviously apply, and it will take slightly longer. That was my backstory. Now
let’s get into the main topic of this video, which is how you can get a job.

Step 1: Your CV

6:13
The first and most important point is your CV. You need to have a nice, well-made CV. It should be a maximum of two pages, not more than that. Make sure the details are to the point and crisp. Don’t
write long paragraphs, because recruiters get 100+ applications each day, and nobody is going to sit and read so much.

Make sure your CV is clear and concise. There is a certain UAE CV format that I’ve been told people usually follow, so I’ll link that CV format in the description below. You can have a look. You can
even make it on Canva; it’s super simple.

You need to clearly mention somewhere in your CV, maybe under your contact details: “Currently in India but open to relocating to the UAE.” You have to mention that clearly because it’s very important
to be transparent with the recruiter, so they know this person is serious about moving to the UAE even though they are in India right now.

7:09
The second option is, if you have someone you trust who lives in the UAE, like a relative or a friend, you can simply add that person’s number to your CV. This time, on my CV, I mentioned my friend’s
number. Whenever she got a call from a recruiter, she would WhatsApp the company’s number to me, and I would call them from my Indian number saying that I’m not currently in the UAE and asking if we
could have a Zoom meeting. That really helps.

Step 2: Job Portals

7:37
Point number two is job portals. I’ll tell you the top three job portals you can apply on.

Number one is obviously LinkedIn. LinkedIn is the primary job portal you have to be active on. I would honestly suggest taking a LinkedIn subscription for a few months until you get a job, because it
really helps and makes your life and work much easier.

Second is Naukrigulf, and third is Indeed. These are the top three job portals you should register on and apply through.

8:10
On LinkedIn, honestly, just reach out to recruiters. What I used to do was, let’s say I applied to a certain company. I would go to the company’s profile. I work in marketing, so I would check who the
marketing manager was. I would send them a connection request or personally message them saying, “I have applied for this position via LinkedIn, but I’m also leaving my CV here.” You can also message
the HR person of the company.

These are small things you can do to constantly increase your chances of getting a job. Please don’t write huge messages or essays. It gets annoying to read so much. Just write a small paragraph, keep
it to the point, attach your CV or portfolio, and leave the message.

8:57
Speaking of Naukrigulf and Indeed, I had these apps on my phone. Even if I was out somewhere and had nothing to do, instead of opening Instagram, I would open these apps and keep applying for jobs.

Basically, you have to be crazy about it. If you really want to get a job and come to the UAE, you have to keep applying all the time. Whenever you see a new opportunity or job opening, just apply.

I reached a point where I stopped reading the job description. Wherever I saw “marketing,” I would just apply. I wouldn’t even read the company name, which is not the most appropriate thing to do; you
should know which company you are applying to. But I had become so focused that I was like, “Apply wherever there’s a marketing job.”

Step 3: Don’t Just Apply, Email Them Too

9:47
Point number three: don’t just apply, email them as well. This is something I personally did.

Let’s say I applied to 15 companies today. After applying to each company, I would make a note of the company name, either in my Notes app or an Excel sheet. The next day, I would find the emails and
numbers of these companies and personally leave them a message, either on WhatsApp or by email, or both.

You might wonder, “Isn’t that too much?” But if you need a job, you have to go out of your way and do all of this. At least with this, you can get an interview. And if the interview doesn’t go
according to expectations, you always have the liberty to say, “No, this is not the kind of job I want.” But at least you can get an interview after doing all of this.

10:42
Honestly, with my personal experience, for the current company I work for, I filled in their application on Naukrigulf or Indeed, I don’t remember which. Then I took their number from their website,
emailed them, and also sent them a WhatsApp message. That is basically how they reached out to me.

What happens here is that you are constantly reminding the recruiter or the company, “Hey, I exist.” There are hundreds and thousands of people applying for the same position, so you have to put
yourself out there and stand out.

I’m not saying you need to spam them. Don’t send five or ten emails. Just send one email, one WhatsApp message, and apply on the portal where you found the job.

Step 4: Don’t Quit Your Current Job

11:33
Number four, and very importantly: do not quit your current job. Please continue your current job while applying for jobs in the UAE.

Let me be very honest with you: it is not easy. I’m going to be transparent. Unless, like I said in the beginning, there are a lot of factors involved in getting a job anywhere in the world. It
depends on your luck, your timing, your destiny, and the amount of hard work you put into it. There are many factors.

You can get a job in one day, or it can take one year. So make sure you are not putting anything at stake. Keep a secure job, and apply side by side. Once you give the first round of an online
interview, the second round, and once you have the offer letter in your hand, then quit your Indian job and say, “I’m moving.”

Step 5: Come on a Visit Visa if Needed

12:36
Now coming to the most practical point: let’s say you’ve been applying for months and months and you’re still not getting a job. It’s fine. Trust me, it’s really fine.

Option B is to fly down to the UAE. Come here on a visit visa and stay here for two months. Take that risk. This is a risk I would tell you to take, because after coming here, your opportunities and
chances of getting a job are much higher compared to sitting in India.

Let’s say while sitting in India, your chances are around 40%. After coming here, they are definitely more, maybe 60%. So if you’re not getting a job from India or your home country, plan everything
out. Come here on a two-month visit visa. Manage your accommodation. If you have a relative or friend staying here, you can stay with them.

There are a lot of videos on YouTube about how to come here on a visit visa, manage everything, and apply for jobs. I won’t mention that in this video because it will become too long. Please go on
YouTube, do your research, and I’m sure you’ll find thousands of videos on how to manage it.

Step 6: Manifestation, Prayer, and Patience

13:45
The last point I want to mention, I’m not sure if it will resonate with a lot of people because I don’t know if everyone believes in this, but I’m someone who really believes in manifestation and
prayer.

I feel that whatever I am today and wherever I am right now is the sum total of many things, which definitely include my manifestation and my prayers.

I think it’s so important to manifest. If you want something, think about it, manifest it, create a vision board, look at that vision board before going to sleep every night and after waking up every
morning. Genuinely believe in manifestation. Trust me, this really helps.

Plant the dream in your brain and start imagining that it has already happened. Trust me, it will happen. You need to be focused on it. Constantly think about it, but don’t be delusional. Pray to God.
I really believe in God, and I believe that praying solves many things.

14:53
Please be patient. I know this entire phase of job searching is very tedious. It can get really demotivating, and it can make you question yourself: “Am I not worthy enough? Why are people rejecting
me?” But let me tell you, it’s not you. There are hundreds of reasons why you might get rejected.

At the same time, I would really recommend that you upgrade your skills. Constantly upgrade your skills. While you are on this job-search journey, do a course side by side, or do anything that
upgrades you and your skills in the field you work in. That obviously helps. It boosts your confidence and your ability to work better in whichever job you get.

15:42
I genuinely believe in timing. I believe that nothing happens before or after its time. Things happen only when the time is right. I’m a big believer in destiny, and I believe that when something has
to happen, it will happen.

The only thing in your hands is to keep trying and trying. That’s the only thing you can do. Trust me, the universe will recognize your hard work one day and give it to you. I’m a living example of
that. Whenever I have wanted something, I have worked towards it, prayed, manifested, and worked super hard for it. Eventually, I have always ended up getting it.

So trust me, if I can do it, anybody in the world can do it. There are so many opportunities here in the UAE. Look at the ratio of people working here in this country; more than the locals, it’s
people who have come from outside. So I’m sure there is an opportunity sitting here somewhere for everyone watching this video.

16:43
But like I said, you have to do your part. You can’t just keep manifesting and not do anything towards it. You can’t create a vision board and not work towards it. Manifestation and hard work go hand
in hand. You have to do both together to finally achieve what you want.

Also, every rejection is a blessing in disguise. This may sound very bookish or like something I heard on a podcast, which I probably have, but I genuinely believe it because every rejection takes you
to what is actually meant for you.

If you are getting rejected 20 times, it’s because your 21st time is going to be your final blessing. Understand why you are getting rejected and work on it.
